# Discovery ledger — 2026-11-15

Window: since 2026-10-25. Channels: arxiv, blogs, cites, ghsearch, github, repoevent, venues. Candidates: 1453 raw, 29 already indexed, **734 new source leads**, **483 indexed-repo events**.

Triage source rows as INCLUDE (digest it), EXCLUDE (reason), or PENDING (what is missing). For indexed-repo events use UPDATE-DIGEST, NOTE-IN-REPOS or IGNORE with a reason. Mark every Decision cell on the batch branch; blank means the sweep is unfinished.

## Triage — 2026-11-16, Claude weekly sweep (branch `claude/sweep-2026-11-16`)

**Counts: 1,217 rows → 734 source leads: 4 INCLUDE, 173 PENDING, 557 EXCLUDE; 483 indexed-repo events: 1 UPDATE-DIGEST, 10 NOTE-IN-REPOS, 472 IGNORE; 0 blank. Plus the agreed carry-over section (64 rows from the 4 and 5 November ledgers that never reappeared): 0 INCLUDE, 20 PENDING, 44 EXCLUDE.** By channel (INCLUDE/PENDING/EXCLUDE): blog 0/3/44, github 4/30/233, pypi 0/0/7, arxiv 0/32/47, venue 0/28/171, cites 0/80/55. The window (since 25 October) overlaps the 2026-11-08 ledger's, so **631 of the 734 leads reappeared** from that ledger: their agreed decisions (merged 9 November) are carried over verbatim and marked as such, except where this run could read the primary (TileFoundry, AgentKernelArena, Xe-Forge moved PENDING → INCLUDE; `tilefoundry` 0.0.4rc1 PENDING → EXCLUDE as the same family). The 103 rows new this week were triaged individually.

**What INCLUDE means this week.** All four digests come from GitHub-hosted primaries read by shallow `git clone` on 16 November: `tile-ai/TileFoundry` (README plus the example reports, `BENCH.md` and `SGLANG_BASELINE.md`), `AMD-AGI/AgentKernelArena` (README, benchmark-methodology reference, task tree), `IntelLabs/Xe-Forge` (README, `docs/CORRECTNESS.md`) and `meta-pytorch/ptxv` (README, `ASSUMPTIONS.md`, `BUGS.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, Lean-model notice). PyPI metadata was read directly. **Every paper candidate stays PENDING for the same cause as on 9 November: this scheduled runner's egress proxy refuses arxiv.org, export.arxiv.org, dl.acm.org, usenix.org, ieee.org, pytorch.org and discourse.llvm.org (HTTP 403 on CONNECT), and the WebFetch permission prompt is unanswered on an unattended run.** The maintainer can allow those hosts for this task (claude.ai Settings → Capabilities → allowed domains); until then arXiv-only candidates — including the Tier A candidates *Agentic AI vs ML-based Autotuning*, Trinity, *Beyond atol and rtol*, FastKernels, KernelAudit/KernelMutate and ModelBlaster — can only be digested by the GitHub Action runner or by ChatGPT.

**Topic coverage checklist (AGENTS.md, Discovery rule 2).** Kernel DSLs and tile languages — checked (ghsearch `tile language`, `tile programming`, `tensor compiler`; org watch tile-ai/NVIDIA/triton-lang/flagos-ai): TileFoundry (digested), *Retargetable Compilation via a Unified Layout Abstraction*, SPMW, TauLang, Tilus, croqtile, NVIDIA tensor-ir, Helion CuTeDSL backend commits. Superoptimization and equality saturation — checked (ghsearch `superoptimizer`, `equality saturation`; cited-by Tensat 2101.01332; OOPSLA/ASPLOS/CC tables of contents): oqed (new), Trinity, *Optimism in Equality Saturation*, XEG, Versioned E-Graphs, Tamagoyaki. Megakernels and whole-step compilation — checked (ghsearch `megakernel`; cited-by MPK 2512.22219 and ForgeMegakernel): MegaOps (new), mKernel (new repo + paper), *Understanding GPU Megakernels*, Mixture-of-Kittens, UniEP, Weave, AutoMegaKernel, latch, megabake; TileFoundry's Nemotron single-launch decode kernel is digested. Distributed and collective compilation — checked (cited-by Triton-distributed 2504.19442 and DITRON; ISCA via OpenAlex): NCCL-BOT (new), Zero-SM NCCL artifact (new), MoE-Hub (new), Entwine, FiCCO, KOSMOS, CommBench, `meta-pytorch/autoparallel`. Autotuning and cost models — checked (ghsearch `autotuning`, `tensor program optimization`; arXiv `auto-tun`, `cost model`; PyTorch blog feed): Helion compile-time heuristics post (new, PENDING), SysPilot, Schedgehammer, Pruner, LLMulator, GPU Forecasters, learned cost-model transfer. LLVM/MLIR middle-end agents — checked (LLVM Discourse core and theme feeds; cited-by LLVM-Bench, PeepholeBench and Magellan; CC/CGO tables of contents): MLIRBench (new), APG, PassNet, *Can LLMs Recover Semantic Optimization Opportunities*, flag selection with small models, `dtcxzyw/tuffy`. Verification and equivalence checking — checked (ghsearch `lean kernel verification`; cited-by VOLTA 2511.12638, RESOLVE and Measuring the Checker; org watch meta-pytorch/facebookresearch): ptxv (digested), KernelAudit and KernelMutate (new), SFU-Sim (new), *Beyond atol and rtol*, Racer, M2K, Triton-Sanitizer, SuperCollider, VOLTA `.ptr` contracts, lean_kernel_verify PTX translation. Benchmarks and oracles — checked (cited-by KernelBench-Verified, KernelGenBench, JAXBench, HPCAgent-Bench, D2K-Bench): AgentKernelArena (digested), MLIRBench and KernelMosaic (new), XStack (new), FastKernels, HPC-Bench, CANN Bench, EdgeKernelBench, GPUPhysBench, KREX, HPCAgent-Bench v0.5.0. Agent harnesses and controller improvement — checked (cited-by CAKE 2608.12629, Kernel Forge, Agentic Harness for Real-World Compilers; org watch deepseek-ai, stanford-iris-lab, gepa-ai, sigilagent): ForgeSystem (new), *Harness Engineering for LLM-Driven GPU Kernel Generation*, AReaL-TIK, KernelBrain, GEAK `mega_mode` expert skills, `dsh-superagent-prototype`, `accelerator-agents` auto-search change. Hardware bring-up and calibrated simulators — checked (cited-by ACT, JAXBench, Prism; HPCA/MICRO/ISCA via OpenAlex; ghsearch `tensor compiler`): openTPU (new), AMARA (new), ModelBlaster repository (read; paper PENDING), *A Case Study of a Multi-Agent Kernel-Optimization Loop That Learns a New AI Accelerator*, *Learning to Formulate*, æSIP, Simthesizer, `tpu-codesign` (excluded as model-design guidance). Serving and runtime systems — checked (arXiv `serving`, `inference engine`; org watch uw-syfi, magnitudedev, flashinfer-ai, nvidia/tensorrt-llm): *The GPU Needs an Operating System* (new), per-step MoE load balancing, FlashVector, Runway serving case study, FlashInfer v0.7.x, TensorRT-LLM 1.4 rc, Magnitude 0.2.x. Vendor stacks — NVIDIA checked (org watch: cutlass, cuda-tile, cutile-python, tensor-ir, TileGym, numba-cuda-mlir, skills, compileiq data releases), AMD checked (AMD-AGI: AgentKernelArena digested, AKLR, GEAK, Hyperloom, Magpie, TraceLens, ProfileLens; ROCm: FlyDSL v0.5.0, rocmlirTriton, hip-ep, tritonBLAS), Google checked (AI-Hypercomputer accelerator-agents and tpu-codesign; openxla xla, tokamax, xla-serving), Meta checked (meta-pytorch ptxv digested, MSLK, dist_moe, tritonparse, autoparallel; facebookresearch sgl-muse-mega, lean_kernel_verify; facebookexperimental triton/TLX, CUTracer), Apache TVM/MLC checked (TVM-TIRx v0.1.0 UPDATE-DIGEST, tvm pin, tirx-harness/kcoral/tirx-kernels rc stream; the MLC blog feed still 404s), Modular — **not checked** (no feed in `feeds.txt`; nothing surfaced through other channels), Huawei Ascend checked (mindspore-ai akg and hyper-multicore, triton-ascend, tilelang-ascend, CANNBot-CAKE, CANN Bench, NPUMeter), Intel checked (Xe-Forge digested; adding `IntelLabs/Xe-Forge` to `repos.md` puts the IntelLabs organization on the watch, closing the Intel gap named on 9 November).

**Channel diagnostics read.** Six OpenAlex venue sources still return 0 works (PLDI 2025/2026, CGO 2025/2026, MICRO 2026, SC 2026, NeurIPS 2026, ICLR 2025/2026 — the DBLP fallback in Next actions is still owed); ATC 2026 404; six blog feeds failed (MLC, TileFoundry, ROCm, Hazy Research, Anthropic, maderix) — the TileFoundry blog failure matters because its 10 October scheduling post and 11 November FSDP example reached this sweep only through the repository; `flashinfer` PyPI 404; the repoevent channel truncated 27 repositories at 8 commits (50 for tvm-tirx and tilelang) and failed on `aetherheart-ai/aeloon` (404, a deleted or renamed repository); `max_repos` did not bind.

**Misses recorded (Discovery rule 4).** (1) TileFoundry, named a miss on 4 November, was on the 8 November ledger as PENDING and is digested only now: the delay is the fetch block, not the channel. Its blog feed is broken (404), so the correct feed URL should be found and `feeds.txt` fixed. (2) Xe-Forge (Intel Labs, March 2026 repository, May 2026 paper) surfaced only through `ghsearch autotuning`, because no Intel organization was watched; fixed by this batch's `repos.md` row. (3) The ModelBlaster paper (3 November) and the `ucb-bar/ModelBlaster` repository arrived through different channels (cites and, by Review-thread search, not the ledger); the `ucb-bar` organization is already watched (`mlirAgent`), so the repository should have appeared as a github-channel row — it did not, which suggests the org watch missed it (created or renamed before the window?) and deserves a check in `discover.py`. (4) `pytorch.org` posts reach the ledger through the theme feed but cannot be read by this runner; the blocker is network policy, not discovery.

The `ucb-bar/ModelBlaster` repository (head `811ef1d`, 30 Oct) was read by clone on 16 Nov: a PyTorch → quantized → Zephyr/RISC-V flow with LLM kernel generation, verify-then-beam-search over spike/FireSim cycle counts, a persistent cache of verified kernels, seven targets including Gemmini and a Saturn outer-product unit, and a multi-model scheduler; the README reports no measurements, and whether the paper describes this repository is unconfirmed.
